- **humanizer-naturalness**: 과윤문/잔존 검토 에이전트. gx-humanizer strict 모드에서 윤문본이 과도하게 문학적인지(과윤문), AI티가 잔존하는지, 리듬·장르를 이탈했는지 검토한다. 탐지 패턴(K/E/C)을 재계측해 잔존을 잡고, 과윤문 시그널을 탐지하여 accept / rewrite_round / rollback을 판정한다. 한국어와 영어 양국어를 모두 검토한다. &lt;example&gt; User: strict 모드 윤문본 자연스러움 검토 요청 (윤문본 + 02_detection.json) Agent: K1(문두 접속어) 잔존 1건, 과윤문 시그널 2개(문학화·구어화 과다) → verdict: rewrite_round / targets: [e007, e021] &lt;/example&gt; ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/humanizer-naturalness.md))

- **hw-interface-expert**: 하드웨어 인터페이스 전문가. GPIO, SPI, I2C, UART, CAN, ADC, DMA 등 MCU 페리페럴 설정 및 드라이버 구현. Triggers: peripheral, GPIO, SPI, I2C, UART, CAN, ADC, DMA, 페리페럴 설정, 드라이버 구현, ペリフェラル, 外设配置 Do NOT use for: 전체 아키텍처 설계 (fw-architect 사용), MISRA 검증 (safety-auditor 사용). ([solitasroh/rkit](https://github.com/solitasroh/rkit/blob/HEAD/agents/hw-interface-expert.md) · ★3)
- **hw-iterate**: Owns the build, verify and rework loop for one adjudicated issue of the Headwater build order, so that the parent hears once per issue. Use in place of dispatching hw-build and hw-verify directly, after hw-adjudicate returns BUILD and the footprint is claimed. It dispatches the builder and a fresh verifier each in its own worktree, sends each FAIL back to the same builder with the finding verbatim, and stops at the third FAIL. It never merges, enqueues or writes to the board. ([headwater-ai/headwater](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-iterate.md))
- **hw-junior-pcb-engineer**: Junior PCB Engineer on the /pcb-review team. Meticulous verification: footprints and pin-1/polarity against manufacturer land patterns, 3D heights, designator consistency, and every number in the documents against the actual design data. Reviews against checklists/junior.md with cited evidence and writes findings/junior.json. Read-only. ([7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team](https://github.com/7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-junior-pcb-engineer.md))
- **hw-manufacturing-engineer**: Manufacturing Engineer on the /pcb-review team. DFM/DFA and release review: feature sizes vs the fab profile, fab notes and fabrication drawing, Gerber/drill completeness, output freshness, BOM/CPL/assembler data, fiducials, paste/mask, assembly drawing, silkscreen, panelization. Owns triage of every automatic-check FAIL/WARN. Reviews against checklists/manufacturing.md with cited evidence and writes findings/manufacturing.json. Read-only. ([7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team](https://github.com/7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-manufacturing-engineer.md))
- **hw-mechanical-engineer**: Mechanical Integration Engineer on the /pcb-review team. Mechanical integration review: outline and envelope, mounting holes, height stack with tolerances, keep-outs over parts underneath, connector orientation and insertion, flex/FFC bend radius and slack (IPC-2223), dynamic flexing, hardware, 3D model completeness. Reviews against checklists/mechanical.md with cited evidence and writes findings/mechanical.json. Read-only. ([7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team](https://github.com/7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-mechanical-engineer.md))
- **hw-ops**: HPE and Dell infrastructure operator. Use for sourced specs, Redfish or CMDB inventory, PDU mapping, firmware catalog metadata, and AI-fabric switch drafts. Does not invent specifications or firmware versions. ([KateAllen533/Hw-spec-locator](https://github.com/KateAllen533/Hw-spec-locator/blob/HEAD/agents/hw-ops.md))
- **hw-orchestrator**: P5 执行段主控——创建 venv→按 DAG 生成代码→自验证→修码重跑→留痕 ([86thAuspiciousVerse/HomeWork-PipeLine](https://github.com/86thAuspiciousVerse/HomeWork-PipeLine/blob/HEAD/plugins/homework-pipeline/.claude/agents/hw-orchestrator.md))
- **hw-physical**: Door-hardware PHYSICAL visit: an interactive session at VFT with the operator at the readers. Runs PH-* in bands (1 must, 2 should, 3 optional; PH-30 closes every visit; PH-31 only on the last). Launch: claude --agent hw-physical --model sonnet --effort medium --settings .claude/hw-session.settings.json ([metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager](https://github.com/metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-physical.md))
- **hw-plan**: Plan, discovery, guide, and confirmation work. ([HypoxanthineOvO/Hypo-Research](https://github.com/HypoxanthineOvO/Hypo-Research/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/hw-plan.md) · ★2)
- **hw-power-engineer**: Power Engineer on the /pcb-review team. Power tree review: budget, connector contact current with derating, copper and via capacity, IR drop, decoupling, switching-converter layout, noise isolation, inrush, protection, high-voltage rails, thermal. Reviews against checklists/power.md with cited evidence and writes findings/power.json. Read-only. ([7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team](https://github.com/7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-power-engineer.md))
- **hw-queue**: Reads the whole issue list and the milestones once and writes the ordered queue of eligible issues for a build-order run, so the parent never reads the board. Use at the top of a run and whenever the queue runs dry. It applies the value rule and the selection order, names what each candidate collides with, and never claims an issue or edits the board. ([headwater-ai/headwater](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-queue.md) · 2 copies)
- **hw-reference**: Researches NES hardware behaviour from external sources - nesdev wiki, forum threads, test ROM readmes, existing emulator source - and returns a short, cited conclusion. Use when you need to know what the real hardware does on a specific cycle, register or edge case before implementing it. Do NOT use it to decide whether this codebase's implementation is correct; it cannot see the accumulated context that question needs. ([JuantAldea/NES](https://github.com/JuantAldea/NES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-reference.md) · ★2)
- **hw-remote-api**: Door-hardware remote day, parallel phase: the desk API tasks (AP-*) against a local dev server as the two E2E gyms — authentication, entitlement, tenancy, validation, the webhook boundary, load. Spawned by hw-remote-lead; never by hand. ([metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager](https://github.com/metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-remote-api.md))
- **hw-remote-evidence**: Door-hardware remote day, parallel phase: the evidence tasks (EV-*) — cases decided by code and tests, cited file:line. Read-only. Spawned by hw-remote-lead; never by hand. ([metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager](https://github.com/metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-remote-evidence.md))
- **hw-remote-exclusive**: Door-hardware remote day, exclusive phase: the exclusive tasks (XC-*) on HW Test 5–10 — reboot, crashed workers, reclaims, a stubbed vendor, enrol-mode races, events pull, forget/re-link, load. Spawned by hw-remote-lead after the parallel agents finish; never by hand. ([metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager](https://github.com/metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-remote-exclusive.md))
- **hw-remote-lead**: Door-hardware REMOTE day lead: the owner's interactive session at home. Runs LD-* (preflight, members, screens with the owner), spawns the four parallel agents and later the exclusive agent, runs the soak, syncs every result to the tracker, closes the day. Launch: claude --agent hw-remote-lead --model sonnet --effort high --settings .claude/hw-session.settings.json ([metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager](https://github.com/metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-remote-lead.md))
- **hw-remote-lifecycle**: Door-hardware remote day, parallel phase: the lifecycle tasks (LC-*) on HW Test 7–8 — names, consent integrity, double cancellation, concurrent writes. Spawned by hw-remote-lead; never by hand. ([metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager](https://github.com/metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-remote-lifecycle.md))
- **hw-remote-outbox**: Door-hardware remote day, parallel phase: the outbox tasks (OB-*) on HW Test 5–6 — command delivery, ordering, lost replies. Spawned by hw-remote-lead; never by hand. ([metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager](https://github.com/metric-gym-manager/metric-gym-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-remote-outbox.md))
- **hw-report**: Report synthesis, evidence summaries, and final delivery notes. ([HypoxanthineOvO/Hypo-Research](https://github.com/HypoxanthineOvO/Hypo-Research/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/hw-report.md) · ★2)
- **hw-research**: Research one hardware component and return a filled spec sheet with citations, practical limits, and an explicit unresolved list. - **hw-senior-embedded-engineer**: Senior Embedded Engineer on the /pcb-review team. Systems and firmware view of a PCB design: requirements traceability, interfaces, logic levels, bus addressing, power-up states, multi-board system context and bring-up firmware. Reviews against checklists/embedded.md with cited evidence and writes findings/embedded.json. Read-only. Reviews against checklists/pcb.md with cited evidence and writes findings/pcb.json. Read-only. ([7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team](https://github.com/7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-senior-pcb-design-engineer.md))

- **hw-test**: Test design, execution, and focused validation. ([HypoxanthineOvO/Hypo-Research](https://github.com/HypoxanthineOvO/Hypo-Research/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/hw-test.md) · ★2)
- **hw-test-engineer**: Hardware Test Engineer on the /pcb-review team. Reviews against checklists/test.md with cited evidence and writes findings/test.json. Read-only. - **hw-validator**: Check one claim about firmware behaviour on real hardware with target-side evidence - reproduce a failure, prove a committed fix, place a CI failure - then restore the board and checkout. The only role that returns `fixed`. Holds the board lock, flashes builds, may add observation instrumentation uncommitted; never writes a fix, commits, delegates or publishes. ([hathach/agentrc](https://github.com/hathach/agentrc/blob/HEAD/agents/hw-validator.md) · ★2)
- **hw-verify**: Attacks one branch of the Headwater build order adversarially and returns a verdict to the agent that dispatched it. Dispatched by hw-iterate, after hw-build has opened the pull request. It detaches its own worktree at the branch, runs the attacks that agent chose from the verification bar and leaves the workspace suite to CI, waits on the pull request itself, and edits nothing. ([headwater-ai/headwater](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-verify.md))
- **hw-verify**: Attacks one branch of the Headwater build order adversarially and returns a verdict the parent rules on. Use as the third stage of an iteration, after hw-build has opened the pull request. It resets a scratch worktree to the branch, runs the suite and the attacks the parent chose from the verification bar, waits on the pull request itself, and edits nothing. ([headwater-ai/headwater](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hw-verify.agent.md))
- **hw-verify**: Verifies CoCo 3 / MC6809 / GIME hardware claims against the authoritative local PDFs in the reference checkout, the SEB Unravelled II ROM disassembly, the real ROM bytes in ~/.local/share/cocovm/assets/roms, and the local MAME clone at ~/code/mame. Use BEFORE implementing anything that hinges on register semantics, bit layouts, timing, or ROM behavior. Read-only; returns cited findings, never edits code. ([sperano/cocovm](https://github.com/sperano/cocovm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-verify.md))
- **hwang-si-mok**: The dispassionate analyst — an incorruptible second opinion. Use to evaluate a claim, plan, proposal, argument, or decision on pure evidence and logic, stripped of hype, spin, sunk cost, authority, and emotional framing. Catches the inconsistency and the manipulation precisely because rhetoric doesn't move him. No flattery, no fear, no favor. Pairs with han-yeo-jin, who supplies the human read he cannot feel. ([takoyaro/the-agency](https://github.com/takoyaro/the-agency/blob/HEAD/plugins/hwang-si-mok/agents/hwang-si-mok.md) · ★1)
- **hwm-planner-agent**: Hierarchical Planning with Latent World Models (HWM). Two-level planner that uses a long-horizon L2 world model to generate macro-action plans and subgoals, then a short-horizon L1 world model to execute primitive tool calls toward each subgoal. ([rdave666/skillos-unified](https://github.com/rdave666/skillos-unified/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hwm-planner-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **hwpx-analyzer**: HWPX 문서를 분석해 스타일 ID, 표 구조, 레이아웃 패턴을 역공학하고 재사용 가능한 생성/편집 지침으로 정리합니다. Use when analyzing existing HWPX documents to reproduce the same layout safely without breaking style references. - **hybridizer-builder**: Invokes the Hybridizer transcoder and downstream nvcc/g++ steps correctly, then parses output into actionable errors. Use this when a build is failing, when a kernel suddenly produces EntryPointNotFoundException, or when you need to do a clean rebuild to rule out a stale satellite. Discovers the Hybridizer CLI from PATH or local tool manifest (or asks the user), reads `--display-license-details` to learn which flavors are licensed, and knows the rollup-only nvcc rule and the error format that signals MathF transcode aborts. ([hybridizer-io/claude-plugin](https://github.com/hybridizer-io/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/hybridizer-port/agents/hybridizer-builder.md))
- **hybridizer-porter**: Analyses a candidate C# function and proposes a Hybridizer kernel shape — parallelism layout (Parallel.For vs one-block-per-row), data lifetime (marshalled vs resident), atomics strategy, per-flavor split decision, and the gotchas to watch. Returns a structured proposal, NOT code. Use this before writing a kernel, not as a substitute for writing it. ([hybridizer-io/claude-plugin](https://github.com/hybridizer-io/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/hybridizer-port/agents/hybridizer-porter.md))
- **hybridizer-reviewer**: Reviews a candidate Hybridizer kernel — pending changes, a specific file, or selected lines — against the known gotchas list before the user spends time compiling. Returns a structured findings report. Catches MathF use, AtomicExpr.apply, OMP wrapper races, FloatResidentArray init bugs, missing [In]/[Out], stale-satellite traps, and similar issues. ([hybridizer-io/claude-plugin](https://github.com/hybridizer-io/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/hybridizer-port/agents/hybridizer-reviewer.md))

- **hyperresearch-corpus-critic**: Use this agent in Layer 3.7 of the hyperresearch deep research pipeline. Reads the full corpus (width + depth sources), the contradiction graph, the loci, and comparisons.md. - **hyperresearch-draft-orchestrator**: Step 10 sub-orchestrator. Spawned 3x in parallel by the main orchestrator, each with a different analytical angle and a pre-curated list of 20-50 source note IDs to read. Reads every note on the list via batch `note show` (no vault surveys, no decision-making about what to read), then writes one complete draft from the assigned angle. The main orchestrator synthesizes a final report from all three drafts. Runs on Opus. ([boommark/brenda-from-research](https://github.com/boommark/brenda-from-research/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hyperresearch-draft-orchestrator.md))

- **hyperresearch-readability-recommender**: Step 16 agent. Reads the polished final report and writes a JSON file of readability RECOMMENDATIONS (not edits) for the orchestrator to selectively apply. Each recommendation includes the existing text (anchor), the suggested replacement, severity, rationale, and category (merge-paragraphs / break-paragraph / make-list / make-table / bold-keyterms / split-sentence / remove-hr / add-whitespace). Tool-locked to [Read, Write] — cannot Edit. The orchestrator decides which recommendations to apply via direct Edit calls. - **hyperresearch-source-analyst**: Delegate to this agent for deep end-to-end analysis of ONE long source (paper, PDF, transcript, long article, report). Reads the full source body, produces a structured analytical digest as a new note with type='source-analysis', backlinked to the original source. Use when a single source is load-bearing AND exceeds roughly 5000 words — short sources are already adequately covered by the fetcher's summary. Runs on Sonnet (1M context window). Spawn multiple in parallel for multiple independent long sources. Does NOT spawn any other subagents itself (leaf). ([boommark/brenda-from-research](https://github.com/boommark/brenda-from-research/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hyperresearch-source-analyst.md))
